[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 1/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='tb.sv')

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 2/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h0E02) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 1"); end
    a = 16'h8003; b = 16'h6479; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h8AB6) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 2"); end
    a = 16'h7521; b = 16'h0003; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h1BB2) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 3"); end

    for (int sa = 0; sa < 2048; sa++) begin
      a = {sa[10], 5'b00000, sa[9:0]};
      for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin b = structured[i]; check(); end
    end
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin
      a = structured[i];
      for (int sb = 0; sb < 2048; sb++) begin b = {sb[10], 5'b00000, sb[9:0]}; check(); end
    end
    ra = 16'h0001; rb = 16'hBEEF;
    for (int k = 0; k < (1 << 21); k++) begin
      ra = lfsr_next(ra); rb = lfsr_next(lfsr_next(rb));
      a = ra; b = rb; check();
    end

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total, errors);
    if (errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product\n\n  // 1. unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  // 2. classify\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)\n  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  // 6. exponent of a normal result\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) else {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  // 8. pack\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='design_v2.sv', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v2 -- binary16 multiply with unified rounding datapath.\n// Key ideas vs. starting point:\n//  * branch decision N0 = exp_sum + msb(prod) <= 35 needs no rounding carry,\n//    and the subnormal path already yields the bit-exact answer in the\n//    one ambiguous case (N0 == 35 with carry): both give min-normal.\n//  * one shared guard/sticky/round computation (windows differ only in\n//    which bits of the shifted value are kept).\n//  * balanced (mux-tree) msb index instead of a 22-deep priority chain.\n//  * Kogge-Stone prefix-OR for the sticky term.\n//  * dead left-shift path of the normal branch removed (normal results\n//    always have msb >= 10).\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[23:16] : |v[7:0];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h1 ? |v[27] : |v[25])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[31] : |v[29])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- shift amounts and branch decision ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;        // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];            // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0  = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  wire       sub = (n0 <= 7'd35);\n\n  // ---- sticky: Kogge-Stone prefix-OR, then one indexed mux ----\n  localparam NW = 22;\n  wire [6*NW-1:0] lv;\n  assign lv[NW-1:0] = prod;\n  genvar gi, gj;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 5; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pref\n      localparam integer SH = (1 << gi);\n      for (gj = 0; gj < NW; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_bit\n        if (gj >= SH)\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj] | lv[gi*NW+gj-SH];\n        else\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj];\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [NW-1:0] below = lv[5*NW +: NW];\n\n  wire [4:0]  sidx   = (sub ? tsh : sr) - 5'd2;\n  wire [31:0] below32 = {10'd0, below};\n  wire        sticky  = below32[sidx];\n\n  // ---- two right shifters, mux after shift ----\n  wire [22:0] prodL = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [11:0] shN   = prodL >> sr;\n  wire [11:0] shS   = prodL >> tsh;\n  wire [11:0] sh    = sub ? shS : shN;\n\n  // ---- shared round-to-nearest-even ----\n  wire        guard    = sh[0];\n  wire        rlsb     = sh[1];\n  wire        round_up = guard & (sticky | rlsb);\n\n  wire [10:0] sum10    = {1'b0, sh[10:1]} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire        cs       = sum10[10];          // carry out of subnormal window\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sum10[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = sh[11] & cs;        // normal-path mantissa overflow\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ? 10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [6:0] n        = n0 + {6'd0, carry_n};\n  wire [6:0] enm      = n - 7'd35;\n  wire       overflow = (n > 7'd65);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (cs == 1'b0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? [ASSISTANT]
v2 passes (144054, area much better but delay worse). Key issues: sticky mux serialized behind the n0 adder, `carry_n` waits for the full rounding adder, and deep `is_zero` select in the pack. Let me create v3 with these fixes:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v3 -- binary16 multiply with unified rounding datapath.\n// Improvements over v2:\n//  * dual sticky muxes indexed directly from sr/tsh (no n0-adder serialisation)\n//  * carry-out cs computed as round_up & (window == all-ones) -- no dependency\n//    on the full rounding adder; sum10 only produces the fraction bits\n//  * exponent pre-offset A = n0-35 computed early (parallel to shifters);\n//    final exponent is a 5-bit conditional increment by carry_n\n//  * overflow from early compare on n0: gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n)\n//  * pack simplified: zero results are covered by the sub branch (zero_in is\n//    always in the sub branch and rounds with cs=0, frac=0), so the deep\n//    is_zero/sub_is_zero select tree disappears.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h3 ? |v[31:28] : |v[23:20])\n               : (h3 ? |v[15:12] : |v[7:4]);\n  wire h1 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[31:30] : |v[27:26])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[23:22] : |v[19:18]))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[15:14] : |v[11:10])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[7:6]   : |v[3:2]));\n  wire h0 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[31] : |v[29])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[27] : |v[25]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[23] : |v[21])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[19] : |v[17])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- shift amounts, early exponent terms, branch decision ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;        // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];            // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0    = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  wire       sub   = (n0 <= 7'd35);\n  wire [6:0] npre  = n0 - 7'd35;       // early; + carry_n gives exponent\n  wire       gt65  = (n0 > 7'd65);\n  wire       eq65  = (n0 == 7'd65);\n\n  // ---- sticky: Kogge-Stone prefix-OR, one mux per branch ----\n  localparam NW = 22;\n  wire [6*NW-1:0] lv;\n  assign lv[NW-1:0] = prod;\n  genvar gi, gj;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 5; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pref\n      localparam integer SH = (1 << gi);\n      for (gj = 0; gj < NW; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_bit\n        if (gj >= SH)\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj] | lv[gi*NW+gj-SH];\n        else\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj];\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [NW-1:0] below  = lv[5*NW +: NW];\n  wire [31:0]   below32 = {10'd0, below};\n\n  wire sticky_n = below32[sr  - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky_t = below32[tsh - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky   = sub ? sticky_t : sticky_n;\n\n  // ---- two right shifters, mux after shift ----\n  wire [22:0] prodL = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [11:0] shN   = prodL >> sr;\n  wire [11:0] shS   = prodL >> tsh;\n  wire [11:0] sh    = sub ? shS : shN;\n\n  // ---- shared round-to-nearest-even ----\n  wire guard    = sh[0];\n  wire rlsb     = sh[1];\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | rlsb);\n\n  // carry out of the 10-bit window + round_up: only possible when window\n  // is all ones and round_up is set -- avoids waiting for the full adder.\n  wire        cs       = round_up & (&sh[10:1]);\n  wire [10:0] sum10    = {1'b0, sh[10:1]} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sum10[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = sh[11] & cs;\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ? 10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow (early compares + 5-bit increment) ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = npre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry_n};\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf  = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_subf = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & sub;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31\n                    : is_subf            ? {4'd0, cs}\n                    :                      enm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan  ? 10'h200\n                    : is_inf  ? 10'd0\n                    : is_subf ? frac_sub\n                    :           frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sy;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v3.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
target_delay has no effect (identical netlists) — RTL structure is the only lever. Now v4: cheap comparator forms for `sub`/overflow, early-increment exponent, and flattened pack logic:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v4 -- delay cuts on top of v3:\n//  * sub / gt65 / eq65 derived from bit tests on n0 instead of wide compares\n//  * exponent = early (npre+1) vs npre selection by carry_n (2-level mux\n//    instead of a 5-bit ripple increment on the late path)\n//  * pack flattened: M = nan|inf|overflow, exp = {5{M}} | (sub ? {4,cs} : enm),\n//    frac = M ? (nan ? payload : 0) : (carry_n ? 0 : frac_sub)\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h3 ? |v[31:28] : |v[23:20])\n               : (h3 ? |v[15:12] : |v[7:4]);\n  wire h1 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[31:30] : |v[27:26])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[23:22] : |v[19:18]))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[15:14] : |v[11:10])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[7:6]   : |v[3:2]));\n  wire h0 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[31] : |v[29])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[27] : |v[25]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[23] : |v[21])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[19] : |v[17])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- shift amounts, early exponent terms, branch decision ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;        // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];            // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0 = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  // n0 <= 35 : n0[6]==0 and (n0[5]==0 or n0[4:2]==0)\n  wire sub  = ~n0[6] & (~n0[5] | ~(|n0[4:2]));\n  // n0 > 65 : n0[6]==1 and n0[5:1] != 0\n  wire gt65 = n0[6] & (|n0[5:1]);\n  // n0 == 65\n  wire eq65 = n0[6] & ~(|n0[5:1]) & n0[0];\n  wire [4:0] npre   = n0[4:0] - 5'd35;      // low 5 bits of n0-35\n  wire [4:0] npre_p = npre + 5'd1;           // early: (n0-35)+1\n\n  // ---- sticky: Kogge-Stone prefix-OR, one mux per branch ----\n  localparam NW = 22;\n  wire [6*NW-1:0] lv;\n  assign lv[NW-1:0] = prod;\n  genvar gi, gj;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 5; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pref\n      localparam integer SH = (1 << gi);\n      for (gj = 0; gj < NW; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_bit\n        if (gj >= SH)\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj] | lv[gi*NW+gj-SH];\n        else\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj];\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [NW-1:0] below   = lv[5*NW +: NW];\n  wire [31:0]   below32 = {10'd0, below};\n\n  wire sticky_n = below32[sr  - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky_t = below32[tsh - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky   = sub ? sticky_t : sticky_n;\n\n  // ---- two right shifters, mux after shift ----\n  wire [22:0] prodL = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [11:0] shN   = prodL >> sr;\n  wire [11:0] shS   = prodL >> tsh;\n  wire [11:0] sh    = sub ? shS : shN;\n\n  // ---- shared round-to-nearest-even ----\n  wire guard    = sh[0];\n  wire rlsb     = sh[1];\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | rlsb);\n\n  wire        cs       = round_up & (&sh[10:1]);\n  wire [10:0] sum10    = {1'b0, sh[10:1]} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sum10[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = sh[11] & cs;\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ? 10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = carry_n ? npre_p : npre;\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire       is_nan = nan_in;\n  wire       is_inf = ~is_nan & (inf_in | overflow);\n  wire       M      = is_nan | is_inf;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = {5{M}} | (sub ? {4'd0, cs} : enm);\n  wire [9:0] frac_m = is_nan ? 10'h200 : 10'd0;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = M ? frac_m : (carry_n ? 10'd0 : frac_sub);\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sy;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v4.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
v4's delay gain was marginal and area grew — v3 remains best. Now a bigger idea for v5: replace the Kogge-Stone prefix + two 32:1 sticky muxes with **shifter-dropped-bit OR trees** (tapping existing barrel-shifter stage outputs — the OR of bits shifted out equals the sticky term). This should cut ~130 gates at similar delay:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v5 -- same structure as v3, but sticky is obtained from the\n// barrel shifters themselves: the OR of all bits shifted out at each stage\n// equals |prod[S-2:0].  This removes the Kogge-Stone prefix array and the\n// two 22:1 sticky index muxes, replacing them with small OR trees over\n// existing stage outputs.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h3 ? |v[31:28] : |v[23:20])\n               : (h3 ? |v[15:12] : |v[7:4]);\n  wire h1 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[31:30] : |v[27:26])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[23:22] : |v[19:18]))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[15:14] : |v[11:10])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[7:6]   : |v[3:2]));\n  wire h0 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[31] : |v[29])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[27] : |v[25]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[23] : |v[21])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[19] : |v[17])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- shift amounts, exponent terms, branch decision ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;        // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];            // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0    = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  wire       sub   = (n0 <= 7'd35);\n  wire [6:0] npre  = n0 - 7'd35;\n  wire       gt65  = (n0 > 7'd65);\n  wire       eq65  = (n0 == 7'd65);\n\n  // ---- barrel shifter with dropped-bit sticky (normal branch) ----\n  wire [22:0] prodL = {prod, 1'b0};\n\n  wire [22:0] n1 = sr[0] ? (prodL >> 1) : prodL;\n  wire [22:0] n2 = sr[1] ? (n1   >> 2) : n1;\n  wire [22:0] n3 = sr[2] ? (n2   >> 4) : n2;\n  wire [22:0] n4 = sr[3] ? (n3   >> 8) : n3;\n  wire [22:0] n5 = sr[4] ? (n4  >> 16) : n4;\n  wire [11:0] shN = n5[11:0];\n  // bits dropped at each stage: stage k drops input bits [2^k-1:0]\n  wire stN = (sr[1] & (n1[0] | n1[1]))\n           | (sr[2] & (|n2[3:0]))\n           | (sr[3] & (|n3[7:0]))\n           | (sr[4] & (|n4[15:0]));\n  // sr[0] drops prodL[0] which is always 0 -- nothing to add.\n\n  // ---- barrel shifter with dropped-bit sticky (subnormal branch) ----\n  wire [22:0] t1 = tsh[0] ? (prodL >> 1) : prodL;\n  wire [22:0] t2 = tsh[1] ? (t1   >> 2) : t1;\n  wire [22:0] t3 = tsh[2] ? (t2   >> 4) : t2;\n  wire [22:0] t4 = tsh[3] ? (t3   >> 8) : t3;\n  wire [22:0] t5 = tsh[4] ? (t4  >> 16) : t4;\n  wire [11:0] shS = t5[11:0];\n  wire stS = (tsh[1] & (t1[0] | t1[1]))\n           | (tsh[2] & (|t2[3:0]))\n           | (tsh[3] & (|t3[7:0]))\n           | (tsh[4] & (|t4[15:0]));\n\n  // ---- select branch ----\n  wire [11:0] sh     = sub ? shS  : shN;\n  wire        sticky = sub ? stS  : stN;\n\n  // ---- shared round-to-nearest-even ----\n  wire guard    = sh[0];\n  wire rlsb     = sh[1];\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | rlsb);\n\n  // carry out of the 10-bit window + round_up: only when window all ones\n  wire        cs       = round_up & (&sh[10:1]);\n  wire [10:0] sum10    = {1'b0, sh[10:1]} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sum10[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = sh[11] & cs;\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ? 10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = npre[4:0] + {4'd0, carry_n};\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf  = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_subf = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & sub;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31\n                    : is_subf            ? {4'd0, cs}\n                    :                      enm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan  ? 10'h200\n                    : is_inf  ? 10'd0\n                    : is_subf ? frac_sub\n                    :           frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sy;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v5.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
v5 traded area for delay (net worse). Best is still v3. Now a new plan: v6 = baseline's fully-parallel two-branch structure (proven lowest delay) + all my v3/v4 cuts (balanced encoder, KS prefix, fast carry-out, precomputed exponent offset, simplified pack):
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v6 -- parallel two-branch datapath (like the starting point:\n// each branch shifts/rounds independently, selection only at pack), plus:\n//  * balanced mux-tree msb encoder (no 22-deep priority chain)\n//  * Kogge-Stone prefix-OR for sticky (shared, log depth)\n//  * rounding carry-out computed as round_up & window-all-ones (the sum\n//    adder is then only needed for the fraction data, not the exponent)\n//  * exponent pre-offset A = n0-35 computed early; final exponent is a\n//    2-level selection between A and A+1 by the rounding carry\n//  * overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry) with cheap bit tests on n0\n//  * pack without the is_zero select tree (zero inputs are always in the\n//    sub branch and round to cs=0, frac=0)\n//  * dead left-shift path of the normal branch removed (normal results\n//    always have msb >= 10; for msb < 10 the carry-out is 0 anyway, which\n//    keeps the branch selection intact)\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n\n  // ---- unpack / classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h3 ? |v[31:28] : |v[23:20])\n               : (h3 ? |v[15:12] : |v[7:4]);\n  wire h1 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[31:30] : |v[27:26])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[23:22] : |v[19:18]))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[15:14] : |v[11:10])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[7:6]   : |v[3:2]));\n  wire h0 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[31] : |v[29])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[27] : |v[25]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[23] : |v[21])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[19] : |v[17])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- Kogge-Stone prefix-OR for sticky ----\n  localparam NW = 22;\n  wire [6*NW-1:0] lv;\n  assign lv[NW-1:0] = prod;\n  genvar gi, gj;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 5; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pref\n      localparam integer SH = (1 << gi);\n      for (gj = 0; gj < NW; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_bit\n        if (gj >= SH)\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj] | lv[gi*NW+gj-SH];\n        else\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj];\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [NW-1:0] below   = lv[5*NW +: NW];\n  wire [31:0]   below32 = {10'd0, below};\n\n  // ---- branch decision and shift amounts (early) ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;   // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];       // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0    = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  wire       sub   = ~n0[6] & (~n0[5] | ~(|n0[4:2]));   // n0 <= 35\n  wire       gt65  = n0[6] & (|n0[5:1]);                // n0 >  65\n  wire       eq65  = n0[6] & ~(|n0[5:1]) & n0[0];       // n0 == 65\n  wire [4:0] npre  = n0[4:0] - 5'd35;                   // (n0-35) low bits\n  wire [4:0] npre1 = npre + 5'd1;                       // early +1\n\n  // ---- parallel shift + round: normal branch ----\n  wire [22:0] prodL   = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [11:0] shN     = prodL >> sr;\n  wire        guardN  = shN[0];\n  wire        lsbN    = shN[1];\n  wire        stickyN = below32[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire        ruN     = guardN & (stickyN | lsbN);\n  wire [10:0] mantN   = shN[11:1];\n  wire [10:0] sumN    = {1'b0, mantN} + {10'd0, ruN};\n  wire        carry_n = ruN & (&mantN);          // == sumN[11]\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ? 10'd0 : sumN[9:0];\n\n  // ---- parallel shift + round: subnormal branch ----\n  wire [11:0] shS     = prodL >> tsh;\n  wire        guardS  = shS[0];\n  wire        lsbS    = shS[1];\n  wire        stickyS = below32[tsh - 5'd2];\n  wire        ruS     = guardS & (stickyS | lsbS);\n  wire [9:0]  fracS   = shS[10:1];\n  wire [10:0] sumS    = {1'b0, fracS} + {10'd0, ruS};\n  wire        cs      = ruS & (&fracS);          // == sumS[10]\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sumS[9:0];\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = carry_n ? npre1 : npre;\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf  = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_subf = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire M       = is_nan | is_inf;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = M ? 5'd31 : (sub ? {4'd0, cs} : enm);\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200\n                    : is_inf ? 10'd0\n                    : is_subf ? frac_sub\n                    :          frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sy;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v6.sv')

Controlled improvements on v3's exponent-critical path.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h1 ? |v[23] : |v[21])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[19] : |v[17])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? shS : shN;\n\n  // ---- shared round-to-nearest-even ----\n  wire guard    = sh[0];\n  wire rlsb     = sh[1];\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | rlsb);\n\n  // carry out of the 10-bit window + round_up: only when window all ones\n  wire        cs       = round_up & (&sh[10:1]);\n  wire [10:0] sum10    = {1'b0, sh[10:1]} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sum10[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = sh[11] & cs;\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ?   -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v9 -- v8 with the rounding adder replaced by prefix logic.\n// The fraction is z + round_up where round_up is a single LSB bit, so\n//   frac[i] = z[i] ^ (round_up & AND(z[i-1:0]))\n// with a 4-level prefix-AND over the 10-bit window (z = sh[10:1]).\n// The carry-out cs = round_up & p[9] is the same prefix's last term.\n// This cuts the carry-chain depth on the fraction-critical path.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? 5'd31\n                    : is_subf            ? {4'd0, cs}\n                    :                      enm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan  ? 10'h200\n                    : is_inf  ? 10'd0\n                    : is_subf ? frac_sub\n                    :           frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sy;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v9.sv')

10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = carry_n ? npre1 : npre[4:0];\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf  = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_subf = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & sub;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31\n                    : is_subf            ? {4'd0, cs}\n                    :                      enm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan  ? 10'h200\n                    : is_inf  ? 10'd0\n                    : is_subf ? [ASSISTANT]
v13 = new best (120911, area 86.14). Now v15: three exact simplifications on top — `is_subf ≡ sub` (proven: sub ⟹ no nan/inf inputs possible), drop redundant `~zero_in`, and 5-bit `npre` (only low bits ever used):
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v15 -- v13 plus exact simplifications:\n//  * is_subf == sub always (a sub-branch decision is impossible together\n//    with a NaN or infinity input: inf/nan force n0 >= 42), so the select\n//    trees use `sub` directly\n//  * `overflow & ~zero_in` == `overflow` (zero operands cannot overflow)\n//  * npre only needs its low 5 bits (enm is 5 bits; overflow uses n0 bits)\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? v16 rejected — v13 stands. One more targeted idea (v17): the 10-bit rounding adder appears on the frac-critical path; replace it with a **carry-select split** — low 5-bit ripple, high half precomputed for both carries (+0/+1 early) and muxed by the low carry-out:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v17 -- v13 with the rounding adder in carry-select form.\n// frac = {zhi,zlo} + round_up:\n//   lo  = zlo + round_up            (5-bit ripple; carry-out lc = ru & &zlo)\n//   hi0 = zhi, hi1 = zhi + 1        (both static in z, ready early)\n//   hi  = lc ? hi1 : hi0\n// This shortens the carry chain into the high fraction bits.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h3 ? |v[31:28] : |v[23:20])\n               : (h3 ? |v[15:12] : |v[7:4]);\n  wire h1 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- shift amounts, early exponent terms, branch decision ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;        // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];            // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0    = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  wire       sub   = ~n0[6] & (~n0[5] | ~(|n0[4:2]));\n  wire       gt65  = n0[6] & (|n0[5:1]);\n  wire       eq65  = n0[6] & ~(|n0[5:1]) & n0[0];\n  wire [4:0] npre  = n0[4:0] - 5'd35;\n  wire [4:0] npre1 = npre + 5'd1;          // early, parallel to shifters\n\n  // ---- sticky: Kogge-Stone prefix-OR, one mux per branch ----\n  localparam NW = 22;\n  wire [6*NW-1:0] lv;\n  assign lv[NW-1:0] = prod;\n  genvar gi, gj;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 5; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pref\n      localparam integer SH = (1 << gi);\n      for (gj = 0; gj < NW; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_bit\n        if (gj >= SH)\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj] | lv[gi*NW+gj-SH];\n        else\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj];\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [NW-1:0] below   = lv[5*NW +: NW];\n  wire [31:0]   below32 = {10'd0, below};\n\n  wire sticky_n = below32[sr  - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky_t = below32[tsh - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky   = sub ? 10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = carry_n ? npre1 : npre;\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf  = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_subf = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & sub;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? [ASSISTANT]
design.sv now reproduces the best result exactly (120911). One insight from this: the 5-bit `npre` cost ~8k product on v13's base — yet v15 (which has 5-bit npre) only regressed by 353. If effects compose, **v22 = v15 with the 7-bit npre restored** could land near 113k. Worth one shot:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v22 -- v15 (is_subf==sub pack, no ~zero_in) with the 7-bit\n// npre restored (the 5-bit form measured much worse on v13's base).\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h3 ? |v[31:28] : |v[23:20])\n               : (h3 ? |v[15:12] : |v[7:4]);\n  wire h1 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[31:30] : |v[27:26])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[23:22] : |v[19:18]))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? |v[15:14] : |v[11:10])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[7:6]   : |v[3:2]));\n  wire h0 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[31] : |v[29])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[27] : |v[25]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[23] : |v[21])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[19] : |v[17])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- shift amounts, early exponent terms, branch decision ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;        // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];            // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0    = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  wire       sub   = ~n0[6] & (~n0[5] | ~(|n0[4:2]));\n  wire       gt65  = n0[6] & (|n0[5:1]);\n  wire       eq65  = n0[6] & ~(|n0[5:1]) & n0[0];\n  wire [6:0] npre  = n0 - 7'd35;\n  wire [4:0] npre1 = npre[4:0] + 5'd1;     // early, parallel to shifters\n\n  // ---- sticky: Kogge-Stone prefix-OR, one mux per branch ----\n  localparam NW = 22;\n  wire [6*NW-1:0] lv;\n  assign lv[NW-1:0] = prod;\n  genvar gi, gj;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 5; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pref\n      localparam integer SH = (1 << gi);\n      for (gj = 0; gj < NW; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_bit\n        if (gj >= SH)\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj] | lv[gi*NW+gj-SH];\n        else\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj];\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [NW-1:0] below   = lv[5*NW +: NW];\n  wire [31:0]   below32 = {10'd0, below};\n\n  wire sticky_n = below32[sr  - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky_t = below32[tsh - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky   = sub ? sticky_t : sticky_n;\n\n  // ---- two right shifters, mux after shift ----\n  wire [22:0] prodL = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [11:0] shN   = prodL >> sr;\n  wire [11:0] shS   = prodL >> tsh;\n  wire [11:0] sh    = sub ? shS : shN;\n\n  // ---- shared round-to-nearest-even ----\n  wire guard    = sh[0];\n  wire rlsb     = sh[1];\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | rlsb);\n\n  wire        cs       = round_up & (&sh[10:1]);\n  wire [10:0] sum10    = {1'b0, sh[10:1]} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sum10[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = sh[11] & cs;\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ? 10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = carry_n ? npre1 : npre[4:0];\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack (is_subf == sub) ----\n  wire is_nan = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf = ~is_nan & (inf_in | overflow);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31\n                    : sub                 ? {4'd0, cs}\n                    :                       enm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200\n                    : is_inf ? 10'd0\n                    : sub    ? frac_sub\n                    :           frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sy;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v22.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
The exploration is complete. **Final result: `design.sv` — area_delay_product = 120,911 (area 86.14, delay 1403.7 ps), 100% correct on all 18,874,371 test vectors**, a **16.9% improvement** over the provided starting point (145,429: area 108.69, delay 1338).

## Summary of the design (`design.sv`)

The final architecture evolved from the starting point through 20+ evaluated variants. Key ideas that stuck:

1. **Carry-free branch decision**: the normal/subnormal selection is `N0 = exp_sum + msb(prod) ≤ 35`, computed without the rounding carry. I proved the one ambiguous case (N0 = 35 with carry) is handled bit-exactly by the subnormal path (both produce min-normal), eliminating the circular dependency the original design had.
2. **Balanced mux-tree msb encoder** (padded to 32 bits) replaced the 22-deep priority chain — a major delay cut.
3. **Kogge-Stone prefix-OR** for the sticky term (5 levels, low fanout; Sklansky's fanout proved disastrous at 1607 ps).
4. **Two right shifters with post-shift mux + one shared rounder** — the sweet spot between the fully-parallel structure (fast, 103 area) and a single pre-muxed shifter (small, slow).
5. **Fast rounding carry-out** (`carry = round_up & (window == all-ones)`) so the exponent never waits for the sum adder.
6. **Early exponent pre-offset** `(n0−35)` / `(n0−35)+1` computed in parallel, selected by carry at the end.
7. **Cheap bit-test comparators** for `sub`/overflow (`n0 ≤ 35`, `> 65`, `== 65`): the single change that produced the best result (v13).
8. **Removed dead logic**: the normal branch's left-shift path (normal results always have msb ≥ 10), redundant subnormal-window shift amounts, etc.

Rejected experiments (all measured worse despite logical exactness): hand-written prefix-AND rounding, carry-select sums, dropped-bit sticky taps, single-shifter consolidation, frac-pack flattening, Sklansky prefix, and various comparator/pack restructurings — synthesis mapping interactions dominated at the ±3% level, and v13's configuration proved to be the best local optimum.

Let me use the remaining steps for one final untested combination: v13's base with only the `is_subf → sub` pack simplification (keeping `~zero_in` and the 7-bit npre):
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v23 -- v13 (= design.sv) with exactly one change:\n// the pack selects use `sub` directly instead of is_subf\n// (is_subf == sub always: a sub decision is impossible with NaN/inf inputs).\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;\n  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;\n  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;\n  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;\n  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----\n  wire [10:0] ma   = {~ea0, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb   = {~eb0, fb};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_e = ea0 ? 5'd1 : ea;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_e = eb0 ? 5'd1 : eb;\n  wire [5:0]  esum = {1'b0, ea_e} + {1'b0, eb_e};\n\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n\n  // ---- balanced msb index of prod (padded to 32) ----\n  wire [31:0] v = {10'd0, prod};\n  wire h4 = |v[31:16];\n  wire h3 = h4 ? |v[31:24] : |v[15:8];\n  wire h2 = h4 ? (h2 ? |v[15:14] : |v[11:10])\n                     : (h2 ? |v[7:6]   : |v[3:2]));\n  wire h0 = h4 ? (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[31] : |v[29])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[27] : |v[25]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[23] : |v[21])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[19] : |v[17])))\n               : (h3 ? (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[15] : |v[13])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[11] : |v[9]))\n                     : (h2 ? (h1 ? |v[7]  : |v[5])\n                           : (h1 ? |v[3]  : |v[1])));\n  wire [4:0] msb = {h4, h3, h2, h1, h0};\n\n  // ---- shift amounts, early exponent terms, branch decision ----\n  wire [4:0] sr  = msb - 5'd10;        // >= 0 whenever branch = normal\n  wire [5:0] t6  = 6'd26 - esum;\n  wire [4:0] tsh = t6[4:0];            // in [1,24] whenever branch = sub\n\n  wire [6:0] n0    = {1'b0, esum} + {2'b00, msb};\n  wire       sub   = ~n0[6] & (~n0[5] | ~(|n0[4:2]));   // n0 <= 35\n  wire       gt65  = n0[6] & (|n0[5:1]);                // n0 >  65\n  wire       eq65  = n0[6] & ~(|n0[5:1]) & n0[0];       // n0 == 65\n  wire [6:0] npre  = n0 - 7'd35;\n  wire [4:0] npre1 = npre[4:0] + 5'd1;     // early, parallel to shifters\n\n  // ---- sticky: Kogge-Stone prefix-OR, one mux per branch ----\n  localparam NW = 22;\n  wire [6*NW-1:0] lv;\n  assign lv[NW-1:0] = prod;\n  genvar gi, gj;\n  generate\n    for (gi = 0; gi < 5; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pref\n      localparam integer SH = (1 << gi);\n      for (gj = 0; gj < NW; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_bit\n        if (gj >= SH)\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj] | lv[gi*NW+gj-SH];\n        else\n          assign lv[(gi+1)*NW+gj] = lv[gi*NW+gj];\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  wire [NW-1:0] below   = lv[5*NW +: NW];\n  wire [31:0]   below32 = {10'd0, below};\n\n  wire sticky_n = below32[sr  - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky_t = below32[tsh - 5'd2];\n  wire sticky   = sub ? sticky_t : sticky_n;\n\n  // ---- two right shifters, mux after shift ----\n  wire [22:0] prodL = {prod, 1'b0};\n  wire [11:0] shN   = prodL >> sr;\n  wire [11:0] shS   = prodL >> tsh;\n  wire [11:0] sh    = sub ? shS : shN;\n\n  // ---- shared round-to-nearest-even ----\n  wire guard    = sh[0];\n  wire rlsb     = sh[1];\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | rlsb);\n\n  // carry out of the 10-bit window + round_up: only when window is all ones\n  wire        cs       = round_up & (&sh[10:1]);\n  wire [10:0] sum10    = {1'b0, sh[10:1]} + {10'd0, round_up};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub = sum10[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n  = sh[11] & cs;\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm = carry_n ? 10'd0 : frac_sub;\n\n  // ---- exponent / overflow ----\n  wire [4:0] enm      = carry_n ? npre1 : npre[4:0];\n  wire       overflow = gt65 | (eq65 & carry_n);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31\n                    : sub                 ? {4'd0, cs}\n                    :                       enm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200\n                    : is_inf ? 10'd0\n                    : sub    ? frac_sub\n                    :           frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sy;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v23.sv')

// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction
// bits) with subnormal support, round-to-nearest-even, canonical qNaN.
//
// Best design (area_delay_product = 120911, area 86.14, delay 1403.7 ps).
// Structure:
//  * branch decision N0 = exp_sum + msb(prod) <= 35 (no rounding carry
//    needed); the subnormal path yields the bit-exact result also in the
//    one ambiguous case N0 == 35 with carry (both give min-normal)
//  * balanced mux-tree msb encoder (no 22-deep priority chain)
//  * Kogge-Stone prefix-OR for the sticky term
//  * two right shifters (normal / subnormal grid), muxed after shifting,
//    one shared guard/sticky/round logic
//  * rounding carry-out = round_up & (window == all ones): the sum adder
//    only produces fraction data, never gates the exponent
//  * exponent = early-computed (n0-35) / (n0-35)+1 selected by carry
//  * cheap bit-test comparators for sub / overflow (n0 <= 35, > 65, == 65)
//  * dead left-shift path removed: normal results always have msb >= 10

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  // ---- classify ----
  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];
  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];

  wire ea0 = ~|ea,  eb0 = ~|eb;
  wire ea1 =  &ea,  eb1 =  &eb;
  wire zero_a = ea0 & ~|fa, zero_b = eb0 & ~|fb;
  wire inf_a  = ea1 & ~|fa, inf_b  = eb1 & ~|fb;
  wire nan_a  = ea1 &  |fa, nan_b  = eb1 &  |fb;

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire sy      = sa ^ sb;

  // ---- significands / effective exponents ----

## Final result — `design.sv`

| Metric | Starting point | Final design | Change |
|---|---|---|---|
| **area_delay_product** | 145,429 | **120,911** | **−16.9%** |
| area | 108.69 | 86.14 | −20.8% |
| delay | 1338.0 ps | 1403.7 ps | +4.9% |
| correctness | 18,874,371 checks | **0 errors** | bit-exact |

The design is bit-exact against the golden reference across all ~18.9M directed/LFSR vectors: subnormals at true value, round-to-nearest-even everywhere (including subnormal results and the N0=35 boundary where the subnormal path provably produces the same bits as the normal path), canonical qNaN 0x7E00 for NaN/inf×0, inf for inf×finite and overflow, signed zeros.

**Why it wins:** the critical optimizations were architectural — a branch decision (`exp_sum + msb(prod) ≤ 35`) that needs no rounding carry, a balanced mux-tree msb encoder replacing a 22-deep priority chain, a shared post-shift rounder with a fast carry-out (`round_up & window-all-ones`) so the exponent never waits on the sum adder, an early-computed exponent offset selected late by carry, Kogge-Stone sticky prefix, and removal of the entire dead left-shift path. About 20 further variants were measured; every additional perturbation regressed due to synthesis mapping interactions, confirming v13's configuration (now in `design.sv`) as the best found: **120,911 area·delay product**.

## Summary

**Final: PASS, area_delay_product = 120,911** (`design.sv`, area 86.14, delay 1403.7 ps) — vs. the provided starting point's 145,429 (108.69 / 1338 ps): **−16.9% product**, −20.8% area for +4.9% delay.

### 1. Approaches tried
- **Baseline**: `starting_point.v` copied to `design.sv` → 145,429.
- **Unified-datapath line** (v2–v5, v7–v12, v16–v20, v23): one branch decision, one shifter/rounder, muxed selection. v2 exposed an msb-encoder bug (fixed), then v3 landed at 123,426.
- **Parallel two-branch line** (v6): baseline-style independent normal/subnormal shift+round paths with all the v3-era cuts → fastest measured (1225 ps) but fat (103 area) → 126,275.
- **v8** (v3 + early `(n0−35)/(n0−35)+1` exponent selection): 122,457. **v13** (v8 + cheap bit-test comparators for `sub`/overflow): **120,911 — the winner**.
- ~20 variants total; best few: v13 (120,911) > v15 (121,264) > v8 (122,457) > v3 (123,426) > v6 (126,275).

### 2. Optimizations with the most impact
- **Carry-free branch decision**: `N0 = exp_sum + msb(prod) ≤ 35` selects the subnormal path without the rounding carry; I proved the single ambiguous case (N0=35 with carry) yields identical bits from the subnormal path (min-normal). This broke the circular dependency in the original.
- **Balanced mux-tree msb encoder** (32-bit padded) replacing a 22-deep priority chain — the single biggest delay cut.
- **Fast rounding carry-out** `carry = round_up & (window == all ones)` so the exponent path never waits for the sum adder.
- **Cheap comparator forms**: `sub = ~n0[6]&(~n0[5]|~|n0[4:2])`, `gt65 = n0[6]&|n0[5:1]`, `eq65 = ...` — v13's decisive gain (area −2 at neutral delay).
- **Kogge-Stone prefix-OR** for sticky; **dead left-shift path removed** (normal results always have msb ≥ 10; wrapped amounts produce carry=0 garbage that's provably discarded).

### 3. What regressed (all measured worse despite being logically exact)
Prefix-AND rounding (v9), carry-select sum (v17), shifter dropped-bit sticky taps (v5, +9 area from full-width stages), single pre-muxed shifter (v10, +94 ps), single sticky mux (v18), Sklansky prefix (v21, fanout → 1607 ps), frac-pack flattening (v12), `is_subf→sub` pack change (v23), OR-form exponent operands (v19), 5-bit `npre` (cost 8k on v13's base!), range-restricted shifters (v16). Effects **don't compose**: v15's combo ≈ v13 despite containing two regressing parts.

### 4. Lessons
- **The flow is deterministic per RTL content, and mapping is chaotic at ±3%**: logically-equivalent or strictly-simpler rewrites routinely moved cost by ±5–10%. Isolate **one change per evaluation**; combinatorial stacking is meaningless.
- A key catch: I nearly shipped `design.sv` with a v15 leftover (5-bit `npre`) that silently cost 8k product — re-evaluating the final canonical file caught it. Always re-verify the exact shipped file.
- `target_delay` had **zero effect** (identical netlists at 150/500/1200) — RTL structure is the only lever here.
- Next time: establish the parallel-vs-serial tradeoff curve early (v6 vs v3: ~10.5 ps saved per area unit — anything cheaper than that on the critical path wins), then hill-climb one edit at a time and treat any unexplained metric jump as a red flag to bisect.
